f2478c260ea39764e72977638ddfa1c38ec445e3 [ diff ] 
[Extensions] Support IsolatedModeForceInstalledExtensionsSettings policy

Previously, force-installed extensions were always forced to remain
enabled in Enterprise Isolated Mode, and the per-extension Isolated Mode
preference always defaulted to false when unset.

Respect the IsolatedModeForceInstalledExtensionsSettings policy for
force-installed extensions in Enterprise Isolated Mode so administrators
can choose whether force-installed extensions are always enabled or
enabled by default while allowing users to disable them.

A follow-up CL will reload force-installed extensions when the policy
changes at runtime.

parent
af1c6f64e4f70158871a34c1891a44d1424a8e10 [ diff ] 
[TrustedVault] Return key versions in StandaloneTrustedVaultBackend

Update StandaloneTrustedVaultBackend::FetchKeys and
StandaloneTrustedVaultFrontend::FetchKeys to pass
std::vector<TrustedVaultKeyAndVersion> instead of raw key bytes.

In StandaloneTrustedVaultBackend::FulfillFetchKeys, strip any leading
pre-enrollment constant key before calling
GetTrustedVaultKeysWithVersions with the stored last_key_version so
returned keys carry accurate sequential versions.

Add a temporary base::ToVector adapter in
StandaloneTrustedVaultClient::FetchKeys until the cross-platform
TrustedVaultClient interface is migrated in a follow-up commit.

Handle guard setting changes in ObjectPermissionContextBase

ObjectPermissionContextBase::OnContentSettingChanged previously ignored
notifications for guard_content_settings_type_, which prevented
permission revocation events from being dispatched to observers when an
origin or default guard setting was changed to block.

This CL updates OnContentSettingChanged to process changes to
guard_content_settings_type_ and notify observers when an origin's
permissions are blocked.

TAG=agy

[Public Transit] Add HtmlElementSpec#shadow() for open shadow roots

- Add HtmlElementSpec#shadow(String) to step into a host element's open
  shadowRoot (chainable for nested shadow roots).
- Add WebPageFacility#declareHtmlElement(HtmlElementSpec).
- Migrate HistoryWebUiRenderTest to a WebPageFacility that waits for
  chrome://history/ to finish loading and for the toolbar search
  field, three shadow roots deep, before taking the screenshot.

Shadow roots are entered explicitly instead of searching all of them:
ids only need to be unique within one root, and a document-wide search
on chrome://history/ matches the cr-iconset <g id="search"> in the
light DOM before the toolbar's search field.

Bug: 428032441

[🌷] Revert "Re-enable SiteSettingsTest#testProtectedContent..."

This reverts commit 8b718b505753df1aaec633d70d47cc29aab7c6f6.

Reason for revert:
Replacing settingsActivity.finish() with
ApplicationTestUtils.finishActivity() in SiteSettingsTestHelper causes
batched SiteSettingsMenuTest#testOnlyExpectedPreferences* to hit the
shard command timeout (ABORT) on android-15-tablet-landscape-x64-rel
and android-11-x86-fyi-rel (~120 synchronous Stage.DESTROYED waits in
one @Batch(Batch.PER_CLASS) run).

Test history: https://ci.chromium.org/ui/test/chromium/%3A%2F%2Fchrome%2Fandroid%5C%3Achrome_public_test_apk%21junit%3Aorg.chromium.chrome.browser.site_settings%3ASiteSettingsMenuTest%23testOnlyExpectedPreferencesProtectedMedia
Failed build: https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chromium/builders/ci/android-15-tablet-landscape-x64-rel/18382/overview

5d395ae1de53b78107368bf17bd70f8f64b66a93 [ diff ] 
Promote on-device model disk checks for foreground requests

Internal testers report that on-device model availability APIs can take
several seconds to return, making them impractical for interactive use.
Investigation traced this variable latency to BEST_EFFORT disk-space
checks that availability requests wait on, particularly under system
load and shortly after page load.

Give the desktop delegate an updateable sequenced runner used only for
disk-space checks. When initialization of the current manifest is
requested, promote queued and subsequent checks from BEST_EFFORT to
USER_VISIBLE until initial asset verification finishes. This applies to
replacement manifests as well as startup, without resetting the broker's
one-shot initialization or its capability snapshot.

Also promote disk evaluations that gate foreground asset registration
and download requests after initialization, including checks already
pending when foreground demand arrives. Track initialization and
foreground evaluation as independent promotion reasons; restore
BEST_EFFORT only after both reasons clear. Foreground evaluation promotion
ends when the disk result is available, not when downloads finish.
Already-ready assets do not require this foreground evaluation promotion.
Disk-space queries now run sequentially on this runner.

Other initialization work keeps its priority.

Remove the switch that forced USER_VISIBLE disk checks
(optimization-guide-get-free-disk-space-with-user-visible-priority-task).
Its only user, on_device_model_browsertest.cc, runs with
OverrideManifestAssetManagerDelegate, which never read it.

Test real disk queries behind a BEST_EFFORT execution fence. Cover
availability and session creation with installed assets, replacement
manifest initialization, and later download requests after disk-space
information expires. Verify that pending checks are promoted and that
overlapping reasons do not demote one another prematurely.

Bug: 581158401
Test: OptimizationGuideOnDeviceModelInstallerTest.*
Test: ManifestBrokerStateTest.*
Test: OnDeviceModelExecutionEnabledBrowserTest.* (compile-only locally)
Test: LogOnDeviceMetricsOnStartupEnabledBrowserTest.* (compile-only locally)

abaf518dfbbbd46dcbfe136f40e42710466fb74d [ diff ] 
file_manager: Fix O(N^2) metadata leak to cut open time by 27%

When a user clicks a folder in the ChromeOS Files app, the file list
stalls while loading. This change cuts opening time for a 100-file
Downloads folder from 198 ms to 144 ms (27% faster, 1.4x speedup).
Numbers are medians of 3 Tast runs on a 2-core x86 Chromebook.

Clicking a folder makes Files app load file details, such as sizes and
timestamps, in batches of 25 files. Previously, finished requests were
never cleared from the pending list. As a result, each new batch
re-checked every finished request from earlier batches and earlier
folders in the session. That turned N metadata fetches into O(N^2) work
instead of O(N).

Remove each request once fulfilled so every new batch checks only
requests that are still in flight.

This CL also adds a new unit test verifying that completed requests are
dropped.

TEST=filemanager.UIPerf.directory_list (key perf test for this scene)
TEST=FileManagerJsTest.MetadataModel

Mon Nov 23 06:08:56 2026
tree
8fd8953320477896002e5eb484c93ad3c452875e
parent
c1e44f4f0fb82686292bca33a38e8feb5ccd25ac [ diff ] 
Rename IsFstEngine() to HasXkbPrefix().

Not all input methods with "xkb:" prefix in their IDs are backed
by FST engine (in IME Service, directly or via VK+IME extension).
Some could be direct-XKB-layout, handled entirely by system
without IME Service involvement. This could be conditional (on
whether assistive features like autocorrect are enabled) or
permanent (assistive features unsupported or inapplicable).

Not all direct-XKB-layout input methods necessarily have "xkb:"
prefix in their IDs either (e.g. b:565986383, b:565990195). As
such, input method IDs generally shouldn't be parsed (see
b:587693588). This rename is just a temp workaround effort to at
least make things more accurate, less misleading and confusing.

BUG=589462840

[3p freshness] third_party/cldr: Fix Version in README.chromium

README.chromium says Version: release-45 and gives a release-45
Revision, but src/ holds the CLDR 44 release data.
https://crrev.com/c/8795452 left both for the next crowbar promote to
rewrite, and that roll will not come: cldr is Static
(https://crbug.com/584852234).

Set Version to 44-0.1, the Debian package version crowbar.txtpb pins,
and drop Revision. This is what crowbar promote writes for a Debian
upstream, and what third_party/usb_ids has.

cannot depend on chrome/browser/ttc. Move the image annotator to

builder wraps the bytes. No behavior change.

[Public Transit] Open tabs through Stations in TabListEditor tests

Replace ChromeTabUtils#newTabFromMenu() in SelectableTabListEditorTest
and ClosableTabListEditorTest with Journeys.createTabsFast(), which
opens all the tabs in one Trip. Its intermediate tabs are appended in
order but created lazily, without a renderer. That's fine here:
- the thumbnail tests use prepareBlankTabWithThumbnail(), unchanged;
- the canonical URL tests load their page in the last (loaded) tab, or
  only read Tab#getUrl(), which returns a lazy tab's pending URL;
- the bookmark tests check that a bookmark exists for the tab, and no
  test checks a lazy tab's title.

Also load pages through the Station (loadWebPageProgrammatically())
instead of the test rule, so the Station matches the tab, inflate the
tab switcher in setUp through the Hub Stations, and drop the no-op
prepareBlankTab(1) calls.

Bug: 428030649

tree
0197d740a3d045033f75015549c47595b546e116
parent
30c353dea096adaaa73b95caca294f6557349c83 [ diff ] 
[TWA] Defer share target POST from DidFinishNavigation to a new task

PendingShareTargetPost::DidFinishNavigation() synchronously called
SendPostAndDestroy() to start the share target POST navigation.
However, DidFinishNavigation() can run from ~NavigationRequest() while
content is tearing down an uncommitted bootstrap navigation—specifically
inside RenderFrameHostImpl::RenderProcessGone() when a pending-commit
renderer process exits before committing. Starting a new navigation
synchronously discards and deletes the speculative RenderFrameHostImpl
still on the stack, causing a use-after-free when RenderProcessGone()
continues executing.

Schedule SendPostAndDestroy() on a new task via WeakPtr instead, so it
does nothing if the pending POST is dropped, sent for another reason, or
its WebContents is destroyed in the meantime.

Bug: 587056010

2026-11-12 ruy-eng ruy: Fix OOM wild pointer, float cache eviction, and ARM asm bugs

Upstream ruy commit 0face1b46 added #include "ruy/check_macros.h" to
ruy/prepacked_cache.cc and updated upstream ruy/BUILD and
ruy/CMakeLists.txt accordingly. In Chromium's third_party/ruy/BUILD.gn,
source_set("ruy_prepacked_cache") reached :ruy_check_macros only via a
private dep of :ruy_mat, causing gn check to fail during
generate_build_files. Added ":ruy_check_macros" directly to deps in
source_set("ruy_prepacked_cache") before ":ruy_mat".

This third_party/ruy/BUILD.gn remediation and description update are
AI-generated (Project Chakra) **and human-reviewed by alejandrori**

Created with:
  roll-dep src/third_party/ruy/src

Test: gn check out/Default '//third_party/ruy:*'

af55c8c1cf7c3abb2f9fa4d87c1f08aa937fc1a3
parent
a3851baebff824565e124e1f5f719346f295989d [ diff ] 
ime: Guard TextInputClient with WeakPtr in OnCaretBoundsChanged

InputMethodAsh::OnCaretBoundsChanged previously dereferenced its bare
`const TextInputClient* client` parameter across four synchronous
callouts (InputMethodObserver fan-out, engine->SetCaretBounds,
candidate_window->SetCursorAndCompositionBounds, and
assistive_window->SetBounds). If a callout synchronously detached or
destroyed the focused client, subsequent virtual calls through the bare
pointer could trigger a use-after-free or a fatal CHECK failure.

To prevent using bare pointers across synchronous callouts, capture
`base::WeakPtr<TextInputClient>` (`weak_text_input_client_ptr`) at
entry, use it for all subsequent dereferences, and re-validate via
`IsTextInputClientValid` after each callout.

Add unit tests covering client destruction during all four callouts.

CQ_INCLUDE_TRYBOTS=luci.chrome.try:chromeos-betty-chrome

b8c1ba45c92f393ca553c0da5c5a7ff4ccc3045f
parent
e218bdfe6f03622ffcefc8503ba8c10b7b5b705d [ diff ] 
mac: Avoid ScopedCGWindowID map collision and erase(end()) crash

-[NSWindow windowNumber] returns <= 0 (typically -1 or 0) when an
NSWindow does not have a WindowServer window device (e.g. during
session teardown). NativeWidgetNSWindowBridge::InitCompositorView
previously passed that NSInteger directly as uint32_t, truncating -1
to 0xffffffff, and NativeWidgetMacNSWindowHost::CreateCompositor
unconditionally registered a ScopedCGWindowID.

In release builds, multiple windows without a window device (or a
recycled CGWindowID before async remote_cocoa teardown completes)
overwrite each other in ScopedCGWindowID's static map. Destroying the
first instance erases the shared entry, and destroying the second
calls std::map::erase(end()), crashing in __remove_node_pointer.

Normalize non-positive windowNumber values to kCGNullWindowID, skip
creating ScopedCGWindowID for kCGNullWindowID, reset
scoped_cg_window_id_ in DestroyCompositor(), and check map entry
ownership before erasing in ~ScopedCGWindowID().

📝 Commit Message (First Line)
Qt: Prevent QT from loading GTK2 alongside GTK3 or GTK4
📋 Metadata
commit
239b3547600858adf7315e10934842d66df3f832
[ log ]
author
Thomas Anderson <thomasanderson@chromium.org>
Mon Nov 23 01:44:19 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Mon Nov 23 01:44:19 2026
tree
5d5644a1af2a2c1469b55b8a09b9d2be2b954110
parent
470c9f3b03b211e8888d17a887078a6adcc4fdd2 [ diff ] 
Qt: Prevent QT from loading GTK2 alongside GTK3 or GTK4

On LXQt, QtUi is used with GtkUi as its fallback, so GTK3 (or GTK4)
is always loaded before QT initializes. When QT picks the gtk2 style
from qt5-style-plugins or the gtk2 platform theme, it loads GTK2,
whose type registrations collide with GTK3/GTK4, and the browser
hangs at startup.

The existing collision prevention only applied when GTK4 was loaded,
and it replaced a GTK QT_STYLE_OVERRIDE with an invalid style, which
makes QT fall back to the platform theme style (gtk2 again on LXQt).

Now, whenever GTK is loaded:
- QT_QPA_PLATFORMTHEME=gtk2 is replaced with a non-existent theme.
- GTK styles from QT_STYLE_OVERRIDE are replaced with Fusion.
- When QT_STYLE_OVERRIDE is unset and the platform theme is lxqt,
  qt5ct or qt6ct, the style from its config file is checked, and
  QT_STYLE_OVERRIDE=Fusion is set if it is a GTK style.

Fixed: 40279052

views: Keep ScrollView's overflow gradient in step with its size
📋 Metadata

468ddab6217d2c70b87cf5d4962acb996439b968

shi yu jie <syujie875@gmail.com>

tree
82f7c03fbaa58ab7adc31a143e39d4860db4dd7e
parent
b1f079742575098678d8af88443f5cdd09dec78f [ diff ] 
views: Keep ScrollView's overflow gradient in step with its size

ScrollView::UpdateGradientMask() places the steps of the overflow
gradient at fractions of the scroll view's current length, so that each
fade covers 16 DIPs. It returned early, though, unless a leading or
trailing gradient was being shown or hidden. A scroll view resized while
its gradients stayed the same kept the steps worked out for its old
length, which scaled the fades by the ratio of the lengths until a
scroll showed or hid one of them. For example, a vertical tab strip that
overflows at 486 DIPs and is then laid out at 756 DIPs fades out about
25 DIPs at the bottom instead of 16, and one that shrinks from 756 to
496 DIPs fades out about 10.

Only return early when neither gradient was or will be shown, so that a
gradient that stays shown follows the current size. The layer's mask is
still only replaced when it changes, and a scroll view without an
overflow gradient still leaves its layer's mask alone.

Add a test that grows and shrinks a scroll view whose bottom gradient
stays shown. In an arm64 macOS build of an M158-based checkout, whose
UpdateGradientMask() is the same, the test fails without this change
and passes with it, and the other ScrollView tests still pass.

Test: views_unittests --gtest_filter=*ScrollView*

Bug: 588064429

Revert "[glic] Succeed show-only invocations on eligibility gate panels"

This reverts commit 8b76c94b8cbdbc0cb2c4f50b96c923388e746ad6.

Reason for revert: Breaks all 9 newly added
All/GlicInvokeGatePanelBrowserTest.* tests in browser_tests on
chrome/ci/linux-chromeos-chrome (is_chrome_branded=true, where
GlicNoWebview is disabled and GlicWebUIContentsManager is used).
OpenOnGatePanel() sets Host::client_load_error_reason_ directly
without updating chrome://glic WebUI state, so
GlicWebUIContentsManager::WaitForLoad() -> OnWebUiLoadComplete()
overwrites client_load_error_reason_ back to
ClientLoadErrorReason::kClientError (10), causing
ShowOnlyInvokeSucceedsOnGatePanel to fail with kClientLoadError
(timeout on success_future) and the other two parameterized tests to
fail their client_load_error_reason() check.

Failure Link: https://ci.chromium.org/b/8664569218119578769

Original change's description:
> [glic] Succeed show-only invocations on eligibility gate panels
>
> A show-only invocation (toolbar/OS button toggle, 3-dot menu, ...)
> that lands on the sign-in panel already resolves as SUCCESS with
> load_reason SIGN_IN (https://crrev.com/c/8668410): the panel is up
> and shows what it should. The other eligibility gates still fail
> with kClientLoadError, so ineligible-account, location-mismatch and
> disabled-by-admin panels count as FAILED in Glic.InvokeResult2 and
> Glic.CUI.WindowEntryPointInvocation.Outcome. On Beta that is about a
> quarter of all non-success outcomes, which hides real failures.
>
> Generalize SignInBehavior to GatePanelBehavior: a show-only
> invocation completes on any visible gate panel, recording the gate
> as the client load error reason on InvokeTerminated so it can still
> be told apart (and monitored for spikes). Invocations that need the
> client (including ones waiting for on_client_connected or the FRE)
> keep waiting on the sign-in panel and fail fast with
> kClientLoadError on the other gates. Offline, unavailable and
> hidden-panel cases are unchanged. Since gate panels resolve quickly,
> CUI 1 Latency.Success / Latency.Failed will shift with the mix.
>
> Bug: b:588032148
> NO_IFTTT=Comment-only rename reference in GlicInvokeError block.
>
> Change-Id: I529617aec9343d6ec59c14ec6c0ec25d6a6a6964

[media] Replace kVp8Norm with std::countl_zero() in Vp8BoolDecoder

4f846b6b19c2d6b83f1ff78d523b9d38d050acf6

author

Mon Nov 23 01:00:23 2026
tree
039a510d9018030691460f3293c83753bcf3a9ad
parent
6a05680073ebe2f5f3f80f401cd0bd1a56ca9256 [ diff ] 
[media] Replace kVp8Norm with std::countl_zero() in Vp8BoolDecoder

After each bit, Vp8BoolDecoder::ReadBit() shifts `range_` left until it
is back in [128, 255]. It took the shift from kVp8Norm, a 256-entry
table of leading-zero counts, which is std::countl_zero() of `range_` as
a uint8_t. Compute it with std::countl_zero() and drop the table, as
suggested in crrev.com/c/8672859.

The two only differ at 0 (the table has 0, std::countl_zero() returns
8), and ReadBit() never gets there: `range_` is in [128, 255] before a
bit is read and the split is in [1, range_ - 1], so `range_` is in
[1, 254] when the shift is computed and fits in a uint8_t.

No behavior change; Vp8BoolDecoderTest passes, and decoding 200,000
random streams with the old and the new ReadBit() gave the same results.

R=tguilbert@chromium.org, tmathmeyer@chromium.org

Bug: None

tree
2e2deb91861cd130b963640149e08dd018c3ef97
parent
f9951da5145e67a0b9a694ee23e2086b34d28120 [ diff ] 
[Extensions] Respect required_buildflags in FeatureProviderBrowserTest

FeatureProviderBrowserTest.VerifyAPIJSONSourceHasFeatureTableEntries
compares the raw API feature JSON sources against the compiled
FeatureProvider::GetAPIFeatures() table, skipping only entries with
"nocompile": true.

On ChromeOS builds where use_cups is false (such as
chrome/ci/linux-chromeos-chrome), feature_compiler.py omits the
"printing" and "printingMetrics" API features because they declare
"required_buildflags": ["use_cups"] in
chromeos/ash/experiences/extensions/common/api/_api_features.json.

Check "required_buildflags" against BUILDFLAG(USE_CUPS) in the test so
features excluded by unsatisfied required buildflags are expected to
be absent from the compiled feature table, and expected to be present
when satisfied.

Cq-Include-Trybots: luci.chrome.try:linux-chromeos-chrome

Mon Nov 23 00:05:10 2026
tree
30d9f9ab6170b992fe61866e44d6dc56c4dc3452
parent
33b63bdbe96f33b0ae11a816f1a8ca735ac4419f [ diff ] 
Gate DesktopNativeWidgetAura host activation notifications on content

When a DesktopNativeWidgetAura's window tree host gains or loses
activation while another window in the same root (such as a
window-modal dialog or Aura child widget) holds Aura activation, keep
that window active instead of activating the content window or
restoring focus, and schedule a focus restore for when the content
window next activates.

Only emit kWindowActivated / kWindowDeactivated accessibility events
and OnNativeWidgetActivationChanged() when the content window itself
gained or lost activation, and guard against synchronous
CLIENT_OWNS_WIDGET destruction clearing native_widget_delegate_ while
weak_this remains valid.

In Activate(), clear any active non-modal sibling in the root when
host activation is asynchronous, and keep restore_focus_on_activate_
set in OnWindowActivated() until the widget itself is active.

[traffic_annotation] Add static_content option to api_identifier oneof

Adds `bool static_content = 12` to `TrafficSemantics::api_identifier`
so an annotation can specify at most one of `api_service_name`,
`legacy_api_url`, or `static_content`.

Updates auditor validation to reject `static_content: false` (returning
`INVALID_STATIC_CONTENT`), supports `TYPE_BOOL` in archive loading,
regenerates test pyproto bindings, and adds unit tests.

2287923f817f96a12a9ebda21465034fc277afa6
parent
a76a871bd7e3c505a2d88d2f52c25e0ce89ee300 [ diff ] 
[Linux] Mitigate keyjacking in the XDG portal file dialog

The keyjacking mitigations from crrev.com/c/8356355 only cover the
in-process GTK file dialog. SelectFileDialogLinuxPortal is preferred
whenever the FileChooser portal is available, so most Linux users
don't get these protections.

Apply the same two mitigations to the portal dialog:
1. Move ActivationKeyTracker from ui/gtk to ui/shell_dialogs so both
   dialogs can use it, and defer the portal request until Enter and
   Space are released.
2. Treat accept responses that arrive within 500ms of the dialog being
   shown as cancellations. The portal dialog runs out of process and is
   closed by the time its response arrives, so it can't be kept open
   like the GTK dialog. Its map time is also unknown, so the delay
   starts when the request is made and restarts when the parent widget
   loses activation, which happens when the dialog maps and takes
   focus.

Fixed: 583186338

WebElement::OpenOrClosedShadowRoot() for non-null WebElements.

This CL removes WebElement::ShadowRoot().

[Autofill] Exclude closed-shadow elements from AutofillEvent

This CL removes closed-shadow-hidden DOM elements from AutofillEvents
to enforce Shadow DOM encapsulation.

Otherwise, elements and autofilled values could leak across V8 worlds.

TAG=agy
CONV=63ec9f22-f69a-4689-9428-5dabd1a5b761

9561ca5b5900c99ef273a85be1bbb324a6f7fe5e
parent
22cbab644cc843ca0e6e5605be3b51acdf75e6b7 [ diff ] 
omnibox: Share the full WebUI popup across a profile's windows

With OmniboxWebUIPopupShareAcrossWindows, browser windows of a profile
share their classic and AI Mode popups (crrev.com/c/8695085). The full
WebUI popup can now move between windows (crrev.com/c/8695319,
crrev.com/c/8778467), so share it too: OmniboxPopupFullPresenter
reports its kind, joins the pool, skips its constructor-time load when
another window has a full popup, and takes an idle one on show or
window activation. With kWebUISearchboxWithoutModelController
(WebuiOmniboxFullHandler), which stays bound to the window that loaded
the page, each window keeps its own.

The full popup is only shown once its page is ready, so Show() can't
create it. When its omnibox is focused and it has no popup, because it
skipped prewarm or another window took or dropped its popup, the
presenter requests one from OmniboxPopupWebUIPoolClient: it takes the
other window's popup if idle, waits while another window's popup is
still loading so restored windows don't each load one, and creates its
own when the shared popup is busy or closed. When a loading popup is
ready, only the window that was activated last takes it; the others
wait until they are activated.

A full popup is not idle while the omnibox holds a draft, while a
ClearPopup() reply is pending, or while a context menu request is
pending.

e58bc898a44992ac3c42701ed5cd0f3ae017e100 [ diff ] 
cct: Restore reparented OTR Custom Tabs before cipher key creation

When Chrome recreates an off-the-record Custom Tab in-process (e.g. via
doRecreateActivity() on a theme or display change),
TabReparentingController detaches the live tab into
AsyncTabParamsManager without setting EXTRA_TAB_ID on the launch Intent,
relying on CustomTabActivityTabController#tryRestoringTab() to restore
it via TabModelOrchestrator.

CipherFactory generates its per-Activity encryption key lazily when
encrypting tab or cookie state on a background thread. If the activity
is recreated before a cipher key exists,
CipherFactory#restoreFromBundle() returns false. While
finishNativeInitialization() already checks
isOffTheRecordProfileUsedByTabAwaitingReparenting(), tryRestoringTab()
still returned null unconditionally on !hadCipherData for off-the-record
Custom Tabs, dropping the live tab awaiting reparenting in memory and
replacing it with a new blank tab.

Allow tryRestoringTab() to proceed when
isOffTheRecordProfileUsedByTabAwaitingReparenting() is true, matching
finishNativeInitialization(). Post-process-death restorations have no
tab awaiting reparenting and continue returning null without cipher
data.

Automation: http://go/massan-jt/0b4346d9-4435-4664-a06a-92a488ccd26f

parent
eadfce26a1b009be2c96982f5d4b1e698f713967 [ diff ] 
WebUI Toolbar: Match Views handling of back/forward mouse buttons

The back/forward mouse buttons acted as clicks on WebUI toolbar
buttons, triggering reloads or inverted back/forward navigation.

Views toolbar buttons ignore these mouse buttons. On Windows, the
unhandled event becomes WM_APPCOMMAND, which navigates the active tab.
On other platforms, nothing happens.

Match this by intercepting these mouse buttons in the toolbar WebView's
PreHandleMouseEvent() so they never reach the page. On Windows, also
send the matching APPCOMMAND to ExecuteWindowsCommand().

The toolbar still gets focused when these buttons are clicked, but
I'll leave that for crbug.com/519327032.

Fixed: 587338064

748807b3d393ab839eef36efcce1936a803f0abf
parent
724049f9a4d75debf91a4971ac08534eb2222e60 [ diff ] 
GTK: Make the primary background color opaque

Some GTK themes (e.g. Orchis) give the window a translucent background.
GetBgColor("") then returns a non-opaque color, which was used as-is for
kColorPrimaryBackground. Foreground colors are derived from it with
BlendForMinContrast(), which DCHECKs that the background is opaque, so
Chrome hit a DCHECK at startup with such themes.

Composite the window background over the frame color, as was already
done for the native toolbar color, so kColorPrimaryBackground is opaque
and matches kColorNativeToolbarBackground.

Fixed: 41486474

tree
aba77b553de06d2739c04b951cc2091d65741bfa
parent
86658cf6e5fe1ccb9aa029544f0f95b6c0ba106d [ diff ] 
[🌷] Fix HardwareDraw ImageReader teardown race

HardwareDraw previously closed its ImageReader from onImageAvailable()
or from the HardwareBuffer release callback while requestDraw() was
still finishing HardwareRenderer.syncAndDraw() and teardown on
sHardwareRequestThreadExecutor. Abandoning the BufferQueue while
RenderThread was still using the surface triggered a RenderThread crash
on Android 17 and Canary emulators, causing
NativePageBitmapCapturerTest#testWithNativePageHardwareBuffer to flake
~21% on android-17-x64-rel.

Detach the surface and destroy HardwareRenderer in a finally block, and
sequence discardFreeBuffers() plus HardwareBuffer/Image/ImageReader
closing on the single-threaded sHardwareRequestThreadExecutor so they
always run after HardwareRenderer teardown completes. Also defer
creating the Renderer until view recording succeeds, and guard against
null images or failed fence waits.

Based on https://crrev.com/c/8424233.

Test history: https://ci.chromium.org/ui/test/chromium/%3A%2F%2Fchrome%2Fandroid%5C%3Achrome_public_test_apk%21junit%3Aorg.chromium.chrome.browser.gesturenav%3ANativePageBitmapCapturerTest%23testWithNativePageHardwareBuffer
Failed build: https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chromium/builders/ci/android-17-x64-rel/40/overview

Bug: 562625946
